# SSX 2026 CSI Division 40 — Process Interconnections Activity List

## 40 01 00 — Operation and Maintenance of Process Interconnections

- Review record drawings, P&IDs, asset history and prior failures for operation and maintenance of process interconnections
- Field-verify service, materials, routing, tags, isolation points and hazardous-area classification
- Perform condition assessment, thickness testing, calibration checks or internal inspection as applicable
- Develop shutdown, bypass, lockout/tagout, drain, purge and return-to-service plan
- Coordinate process safety review, permits and owner operations
- Submit repair materials, procedures, welding qualifications and test plan
- Isolate, clean, decontaminate and make the affected system safe
- Remove failed components and preserve reusable equipment
- Repair, rehabilitate or replace operation and maintenance of process interconnections by system and area
- Complete examination, pressure testing, calibration and functional checks
- Restore insulation, coatings, identification and protective systems
- Correct deficiencies, update records and return the system to controlled operation

## 40 05 00 — Common Work Results for Process Interconnections

- Confirm service conditions, design pressure, temperature, materials and limits for common work results for process interconnections
- Review P&IDs, piping classes, equipment connections, layouts and existing conditions
- Coordinate process safety, hazardous materials, access and shutdown requirements
- Prepare product data, calculations, shop drawings and installation procedures
- Approve material traceability, joining, welding, cleaning and examination requirements
- Release and receive long-lead materials, valves, actuators and specialties
- Conduct preparatory quality-control meeting for common work results for process interconnections
- Verify supports, penetrations, foundations, clearances and predecessor work
- Fabricate, install and identify common work results for process interconnections by system, line number and area
- Perform dimensional, weld, coating, cleanliness and material inspections
- Complete pressure, leakage, operational or proof testing as applicable
- Correct deficiencies and submit test records, certificates and as-built information

## 40 06 00 — Schedules for Process Interconnections

- Identify cleaning, testing, insulation, coating and identification requirements
- Assign procurement, fabrication, installation and startup milestones
- Coordinate schedule entries with equipment and packaged-system vendors
- Resolve conflicts, duplicates and missing identifiers
- Issue approved schedule for construction and controls configuration
- Update schedule for approved field changes
- Link final schedule entries to commissioning and turnover records

## 40 10 00 — Gas and Vapor Process Piping and Ductwork

- Verify process basis, fluid or material properties, line list, P&IDs and piping class for gas and vapor process piping and ductwork
- Complete stress, flexibility, surge, pressure-drop and support review as applicable
- Coordinate process hazards, ventilation, detection, containment and emergency isolation
- Submit pipe, fittings, valves, supports, joining, cleaning and testing procedures
- Approve welder, brazer, fusion or bonding qualifications and sample joints
- Release material with required traceability, certifications and cleanliness controls
- Prefabricate and inspect gas and vapor process piping and ductwork spools or assemblies
- Verify equipment nozzles, sleeves, supports, slopes, clearances and access
- Install gas and vapor process piping and ductwork by line number, system and area
- Complete weld examination, joint inspection, flushing, blowing, pigging or cleaning as applicable
- Perform pressure, vacuum and leakage testing
- Restore coatings, insulation, heat tracing and identification
- Correct deficiencies, reinstate the process and submit turnover records

- Complete stress, flexibility, surge, pressure-drop and support review as applicable
- Coordinate process hazards, ventilation, detection, containment and emergency isolation
- Submit pipe, fittings, valves, supports, joining, cleaning and testing procedures
- Approve welder, brazer, fusion or bonding qualifications and sample joints
- Release material with required traceability, certifications and cleanliness controls
- Prefabricate and inspect non-potable plant water process piping spools or assemblies
- Verify equipment nozzles, sleeves, supports, slopes, clearances and access
- Install non-potable plant water process piping by line number, system and area
- Complete weld examination, joint inspection, flushing, blowing, pigging or cleaning as applicable
- Perform pressure, vacuum and leakage testing
- Restore coatings, insulation, heat tracing and identification
- Correct deficiencies, reinstate the process and submit turnover records

## 40 34 50 — Dilute Phase Pneumatic Conveying Lines

- Verify process basis, fluid or material properties, line list, P&IDs and piping class for dilute phase pneumatic conveying lines
- Complete stress, flexibility, surge, pressure-drop and support review as applicable
- Coordinate process hazards, ventilation, detection, containment and emergency isolation
- Submit pipe, fittings, valves, supports, joining, cleaning and testing procedures
- Approve welder, brazer, fusion or bonding qualifications and sample joints
- Release material with required traceability, certifications and cleanliness controls
- Prefabricate and inspect dilute phase pneumatic conveying lines spools or assemblies
- Verify equipment nozzles, sleeves, supports, slopes, clearances and access
- Install dilute phase pneumatic conveying lines by line number, system and area
- Complete weld examination, joint inspection, flushing, blowing, pigging or cleaning as applicable
- Perform conveying and blockage testing
- Restore coatings, insulation, heat tracing and identification
- Correct deficiencies, reinstate the process and submit turnover records

- Correct drift, noise, leakage, orientation and configuration deficiencies
- Record final calibration, parameters, setpoints and asset information

## 40 80 00 — Commissioning of Process Systems

- Approve commissioning plan, system boundaries, prerequisites and acceptance criteria for commissioning of process systems
- Verify construction completion, approved submittals and test documentation
- Confirm piping cleanliness, pressure tests, lubrication, calibration and electrical readiness
- Complete mechanical completion walkdown and punch classification
- Verify control narratives, cause-and-effect, alarms, trips and permissives
- Perform pre-startup safety review and authorize introduction of process media
- Execute dry, water-batch or inert-media functional testing as applicable
- Start systems in approved sequence and stabilize operation
- Perform capacity, efficiency, control-loop and integrated process tests
- Test normal, standby, emergency, loss-of-utility and recovery modes
- Trend results, document deficiencies and repeat failed tests
- Train operations and maintenance personnel
- Deliver commissioning report, baselines, backups, manuals and final records
